Answer\n  about what you were given instead.\n- Don't deny what [Targeted Info] says. Hi, Doctor. Thanks so much for seeing me. Um, I hope I'm in the right place, but... I guess the main thing that finally made me come in—and it's a little embarrassing to admit, honestly—is that my eating habits have just been kind of all over the place lately. 

I don't want to make a huge deal out of it, but it actually kind of scared me into making the appointment. Um, well... I really don't want you to think I'm completely letting myself go or anything. I usually try to take pretty good care of myself. But... honestly, that's kind of the main thing that scared me into actually coming in to see you.

Um, well... I definitely don't want to sound lazy or anything. I usually pride myself on working really hard, especially with my clients. But, um... yeah, I guess I have been feeling a bit run down.

========== OUTPUT [patient] ==========
Um, well... I really don't want to sound like I'm just giving up on things or being a bad boyfriend. I usually try to be really active and involved, you know? But, um... I guess I'd have to say it's more like a complete loss of interest lately. 

I used to love playing soccer on the weekends, and I'd always cook nice dinners for my girlfriend. We'd even go check out small art galleries, and at work, I'd always try to help out the newer sales guys. But recently... I just can't seem to make myself care about any of it. It's like the drive to do those things is just gone. 

I feel terrible admitting that, especially about my girlfriend and the guys at work. I hope you don't think I'm just being selfish or lazy. Is it normal to just stop caring about things you usually love when you're feeling this way? I really hope I'm explaining this right for you.

Um, well... I really hope this doesn't make me sound like I'm just sitting around feeling sorry for myself all day. I usually try to keep a really positive attitude, especially for my girlfriend and for the guys at work. I don't want to be a drag to be around, you know?
